530: 
531:         /// <summary>
532:         /// Called when CEF renders a new frame. Copies pixel data and converts BGRA to RGBA.
533:         /// Uses double buffering to prevent color flickering during rendering.
534:         /// Optimized with ArrayPool for reduced GC pressure.
535:         /// </summary>
536:         /// <param name="buffer">Pointer to the pixel buffer in BGRA format.</param>
537:         /// <param name="width">Width of the rendered frame.</param>
538:         /// <param name="height">Height of the rendered frame.</param>
539:         /// <param name="dirtyRects">Array of dirty rectangles that need repainting.</param>
540:         internal void OnPaint(IntPtr buffer, int width, int height, CefRectangle[] dirtyRects)
541:         {
542:             if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return;
543:             
544:             int bufferSize = width * height * 4;
545:             
546:             bool lockTaken = false;
547:             try
548:             {
549:                 _spinLock.Enter(ref lockTaken);
550:                 
551:                 _width = width;
552:                 _height = height;
553:                 
554:                 if (_pixelBuffer == null || _pixelBufferSize != bufferSize)
555:                 {
556:                     if (_pixelBuffer != null && _pixelBufferSize > 0)
557:                     {
558:                         ArrayPool<byte>.Shared.Return(_pixelBuffer);
559:                     }
560:                     _pixelBuffer = ArrayPool<byte>.Shared.Rent(bufferSize);
561:                     _pixelBufferSize = bufferSize;
562:                 }
563: 
564:                 Marshal.Copy(buffer, _pixelBuffer, 0, bufferSize);
565:                 ConvertBgraToRgba(_pixelBuffer, width * height);
566:                 _isDirty = true;
567:             }
568:             finally
569:             {
570:                 if (lockTaken) _spinLock.Exit();
571:             }
572:         }
573: 
574:         /// <summary>
575:         /// Converts BGRA pixel data to RGBA format using SIMD when available.
576:         /// Supports AVX2 (8 pixels at once) and SSSE3 (4 pixels at once).
577:         /// </summary>
578:         [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
579:         private static unsafe void ConvertBgraToRgba(byte[] buffer, int pixelCount)
580:         {
581:             if (Avx2.IsSupported)
582:             {
583:                 int vectorSize = 32;
584:                 int vectorCount = pixelCount / 8;
585:                 
586:                 var shuffleMask = Vector256.Create(
587:                     (byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15,
588:                     (byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15
589:                 );
590:                 
591:                 fixed (byte* ptr = buffer)
592:                 {
593:                     for (int i = 0; i < vectorCount; i++)
594:                     {
595:                         int offset = i * vectorSize;
596:                         var data = Avx.LoadVector256(ptr + offset);
597:                         var shuffled = Avx2.Shuffle(data, shuffleMask);
598:                         Avx.Store(ptr + offset, shuffled);
599:                     }
600:                     
601:                     for (int i = vectorCount * 8; i < pixelCount; i++)
602:                     {
603:                         int offset = i * 4;
604:                         byte b = ptr[offset];
605:                         ptr[offset] = ptr[offset + 2];
606:                         ptr[offset + 2] = b;
607:                     }
608:                 }
609:             }
610:             else if (Ssse3.IsSupported)
611:             {
612:                 int vectorSize = 16;
613:                 int vectorCount = pixelCount / 4;
614:                 
615:                 var shuffleMask = Vector128.Create((byte)2, 1, 0, 3, 6, 5, 4, 7, 10, 9, 8, 11, 14, 13, 12, 15);
616:                 
617:                 fixed (byte* ptr = buffer)
618:                 {
619:                     for (int i = 0; i < vectorCount; i++)
620:                     {
621:                         int offset = i * vectorSize;
622:                         var data = Sse2.LoadVector128(ptr + offset);
623:                         var shuffled = Ssse3.Shuffle(data, shuffleMask);
624:                         Sse2.Store(ptr + offset, shuffled);
625:                     }
626:                     
627:                     for (int i = vectorCount * 4; i < pixelCount; i++)
628:                     {
629:                         int offset = i * 4;
630:                         byte b = ptr[offset];
631:                         ptr[offset] = ptr[offset + 2];
632:                         ptr[offset + 2] = b;
633:                     }
634:                 }
635:             }
636:             else
637:             {
638:                 for (int i = 0; i < pixelCount; i++)
639:                 {
640:                     int offset = i * 4;
641:                     byte b = buffer[offset];
642:                     buffer[offset] = buffer[offset + 2];
643:                     buffer[offset + 2] = b;
644:                 }
645:             }
646:         }
647: 
648:         /// <summary>
649:         /// Called every frame. Updates the texture with new pixel data and handles browser creation.
650:         /// Uses double buffering with ArrayPool for reduced GC pressure.
651:         /// Implements a delayed resize mechanism to prevent flickering during rapid window resizing.
652:         /// </summary>
653:         public override void _Process(double delta)
654:         {
655:             _cachedGlobalPosition = GlobalPosition;
656:             _cachedContentScale = DisplayServer.ScreenGetScale();
657: 
658:             // ── 嵌入模式：每帧同步 CEF 子窗口位置，跳过 OSR 纹理更新 ──
659:             if (_embeddedMode)
660:             {
661:                 ProcessEmbeddedMode(delta);
662:                 return;
663:             }
664: 
665:             if (_browserHost != null && Size.X > 0 && Size.Y > 0)
666:             {
667:                 int newWidth = (int)Size.X;
668:                 int newHeight = (int)Size.Y;
669:                 
670:                 if (newWidth != _controlWidth || newHeight != _controlHeight)
671:                 {
672:                     _controlWidth = newWidth;
673:                     _controlHeight = newHeight;
674:                     _pendingWidth = newWidth;
675:                     _pendingHeight = newHeight;
676:                     _resizeStableCount = 0;
677:                     QueueRedraw();
678:                 }
679:                 else if (_pendingWidth > 0 && _pendingHeight > 0)
680:                 {
681:                     _resizeStableCount++;
682:                     if (_resizeStableCount >= ResizeStableThreshold)
683:                     {
684:                         _browserHost.WasResized();
685:                         _browserHost.Invalidate(CefPaintElementType.View);
686:                         _pendingWidth = 0;
687:                         _pendingHeight = 0;
688:                     }
689:                 }
690:                 else if (_width != _controlWidth || _height != _controlHeight)
691:                 {
692:                     _browserHost.Invalidate(CefPaintElementType.View);
693:                 }
694:             }
695: 
696:             if (_isDirty && _pixelBuffer != null && _width > 0 && _height > 0)
697:             {
698:                 int expectedBufferSize = _width * _height * 4;
699:                 if (_pixelBufferSize >= expectedBufferSize)
700:                 {
701:                     if (_renderBuffer == null || _renderBufferSize != expectedBufferSize)
702:                     {
703:                         _renderBuffer = new byte[expectedBufferSize];
704:                         _renderBufferSize = expectedBufferSize;
705:                     }
706:                     
707:                     bool lockTaken = false;
708:                     try
709:                     {
710:                         _spinLock.Enter(ref lockTaken);
711:                         Buffer.BlockCopy(_pixelBuffer, 0, _renderBuffer, 0, expectedBufferSize);
712:                     }
713:                     finally
714:                     {
715:                         if (lockTaken) _spinLock.Exit();
716:                     }
717:                     
718:                     if (_texture.GetSize().X != _width || _texture.GetSize().Y != _height)
719:                     {
720:                         _image.SetData(_width, _height, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, _renderBuffer);
721:                         _texture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(_image);
722:                     }
723:                     else
724:                     {
725:                         _image.SetData(_width, _height, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, _renderBuffer);
726:                         _texture.Update(_image);
727:                     }
728:                     QueueRedraw();
729:                 }
730:                 _isDirty = false;
731:             }
732: 
733:             if (!_browserCreated && Size.X > 0 && Size.Y > 0)
734:             {
735:                 CreateBrowserDeferred();
736:             }
737:         }
738: 
739:         /// <summary>
740:         /// Called when the control needs to be redrawn. Draws the browser texture.
741:         /// Uses control size for drawing to ensure proper scaling during resize operations.
742:         /// In embedded mode, the CEF child window renders itself — no Godot drawing needed.
743:         /// </summary>
744:         public override void _Draw()
745:         {
746:             // 嵌入模式下，CEF 子窗口自行渲染，不需要 Godot 绘制
747:             if (_embeddedMode)
748:                 return;
749: 
750:             if (_texture != null && _controlWidth > 0 && _controlHeight > 0)
751:             {
752:                 if (Transparent)
753:                 {
754:                     DrawTextureRect(_texture, new Rect2(Vector2.Zero, _controlWidth, _controlHeight), false, Colors.White, false);
755:                 }
756:                 else
757:                 {
758:                     if (_width == _controlWidth && _height == _controlHeight)
759:                     {
760:                         DrawTexture(_texture, Vector2.Zero);
761:                     }
762:                     else
763:                     {
764:                         DrawTextureRect(_texture, new Rect2(Vector2.Zero, _controlWidth, _controlHeight), false);
765:                     }
766:                 }
767:             }
768:         }
769: 
770:         /// <summary>
771:         /// Handles input events from Godot and forwards them to the browser.
772:         /// </summary>
773:         public override void _GuiInput(InputEvent @event)
774:         {
775:             // 嵌入模式下，CEF 子窗口直接接收原生输入，不需要 Godot 转发
776:             if (_browserHost == null || _embeddedMode)
777:                 return;
778: 
779:             switch (@event)
780:             {
781:                 case InputEventMouseMotion mouseMotion:
782:                     SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseMotion);
783:                     break;
784:                 case InputEventMouseButton mouseButton:
785:                     SendMouseButtonEvent(mouseButton);
786:                     break;
787:                 case InputEventKey key:
788:                     SendKeyEvent(key);
789:                     break;
790:             }
791:         }
792: 
793:         /// <summary>
794:         /// Sends a mouse move event to the browser.
795:         /// </summary>
796:         private void SendMouseMoveEvent(InputEventMouseMotion e)
797:         {
798:             if (_browserHost == null) return;
799:

815:             _browserHost.SendMouseMoveEvent(mouseEvent, false);

822:         {

826:                 CefMouseButtonType.Right => CefEventFlags.RightMouseButton,

832:         /// <summary>
833:         /// Sends a mouse button event to the browser, including click counting for double-click detection.
834:         /// </summary>
835:         private void SendMouseButtonEvent(InputEventMouseButton e)
836:         {
837:             if (_browserHost == null) return;
838:             
839:             var localPos = GetLocalMousePosition();
840:             var mouseEvent = new CefMouseEvent
841:             {
842:                 X = (int)localPos.X,
843:                 Y = (int)localPos.Y,
844:                 Modifiers = GetModifiers(e)
845:             };
846: 
847:             var button = ConvertMouseButton(e.ButtonIndex);
848: 
849:             if (e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelUp || e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelDown)
850:             {
851:                 int delta = e.ButtonIndex == MouseButton.WheelUp ? 120 : -120;
852:                 _browserHost.SendMouseWheelEvent(mouseEvent, 0, delta);
853:                 return;
854:             }
855: 
856:             if (button == (CefMouseButtonType)(-1))
857:                 return;
858: 
859:             if (e.Pressed)
860:             {
861:                 var currentTime = Time.GetTicksMsec() / 1000.0;
862:                 if (currentTime - _lastClickTime < DoubleClickInterval)
863:                 {
864:                     _clickCount++;
865:                 }
866:                 else
867:                 {
868:                     _clickCount = 1;
869:                 }
870:                 _lastClickTime = currentTime;
871:                 
872:                 _pressedButton = button;
873:                 _isMousePressed = true;
874:                 _browserHost.SendMouseClickEvent(mouseEvent, button, false, _clickCount);
875:                 GrabFocus();
876:                 _browserHost?.SetFocus(true);
877:                 ActivateIme();
878:             }
879:             else
880:             {
881:                 _isMousePressed = false;
882:                 _pressedButton = (CefMouseButtonType)(-1);
883:                 _browserHost.SendMouseClickEvent(mouseEvent, button, true, 1);
884:             }
885:         }

1142:         /// <summary>
1143:         /// Evaluates JavaScript code in the browser frame and returns the result.
1144:         /// Uses CEF IPC (SendProcessMessage) to communicate with the renderer process.
1145:         /// Supports optional timeout.  Throws TimeoutException on timeout.
1146:         /// </summary>
1147:         public Task<T> EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, string url = null, int line = 1, TimeSpan? timeout = null)
1148:         {
1149:             var frame = _browser?.GetMainFrame();
1150:             if (frame == null)
1151:                 return Task.FromResult<T>(default);
1152: 
1153:             var taskId = Interlocked.Increment(ref _lastEvalTaskId);
1154:             var tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<string>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
1155:             _pendingEvals.TryAdd(taskId, tcs);
1156: 
1157:             var msg = CefProcessMessage.Create("JsEvaluationRequest");
1158:             using (var args = msg.Arguments)
1159:             {
1160:                 args.SetInt(0, taskId);
1161:                 // WrapScriptForEvaluation 生成的函数没有 return 关键字:
1162:                 //   cefglue.evaluateScript(function() { <code>\n})
1163:                 // 所以注入 return 使函数返回表达式结果
1164:                 args.SetString(1, $"return {code};");
1165:                 args.SetString(2, url ?? "about:blank");
1166:                 args.SetInt(3, line);
1167:             }
1168:             frame.SendProcessMessage(CefProcessId.Renderer, msg);
1169: 
1170:             var pending = tcs.Task;
1171:             if (timeout.HasValue)
1172:             {
1173:                 return Task.WhenAny(pending, Task.Delay(timeout.Value))
1174:                     .ContinueWith(t =>
1175:                     {
1176:                         if (t.Result != pending)
1177:                         {
1178:                             _pendingEvals.TryRemove(taskId, out _);
1179:                             throw new TimeoutException($"JavaScript evaluation timed out after {timeout.Value.TotalMilliseconds}ms");
1180:                         }
1181:                         return DeserializeEvalResult<T>(pending.Result);
1182:                     });
1183:             }
1184: 
1185:             return pending.ContinueWith(t => DeserializeEvalResult<T>(t.Result));
1186:         }
1187: 
1188:         /// <summary>
1189:         /// Registers a C# object so its public methods are callable from JavaScript.
1190:         /// After registration, JS can call window.&lt;name&gt;.methodName(jsonArg).
1191:         /// The method receives a single JSON string argument representing the JS arguments array.
1192:         /// </summary>
1193:         public void RegisterJavascriptObject(object target, string name)
1194:         {
1195:             if (_browser == null || _browser.GetMainFrame() == null)
1196:             {
1197:                 GD.PrintErr("[CefGlueControl] Cannot register object: browser not initialized");
1198:                 return;
1199:             }
1200: 
1201:             var reg = new RegisteredObject(target);
1202:             if (!_registeredObjects.TryAdd(name, reg))
1203:             {
1204:                 GD.Print($"[CefGlueControl] Object '{name}' already registered, updating");
1205:                 _registeredObjects[name] = reg;
1206:             }
1207: 
1208:             // Notify the renderer process (CefGlue.BrowserProcess) to create V8 bindings.
1209:             var msg = CefProcessMessage.Create("NativeObjectRegistrationRequest");
1210:             using (var args = msg.Arguments)
1211:             {
1212:                 args.SetString(0, name);
1213:                 args.SetString(1, JsonSerializer.Serialize(reg.MethodNames));
1214:             }
1215:             _browser.GetMainFrame().SendProcessMessage(CefProcessId.Renderer, msg);
1216: 
1217:             GD.Print($"[CefGlueControl] Registered object '{name}' with {reg.MethodNames.Length} methods");
1218:         }

1224:         {
1225:             _registeredObjects.TryRemove(name, out _);
1226: 
1227:             if (_browser?.GetMainFrame() != null)
1228:             {
1229:                 var msg = CefProcessMessage.Create("NativeObjectUnregistrationRequest");
1230:                 using (var args = msg.Arguments)
1231:                 {
1232:                     args.SetString(0, name);

1234:                 _browser.GetMainFrame().SendProcessMessage(CefProcessId.Renderer, msg);
1235:             }

1238:         // ── IPC message dispatch (called from GodotCefClient) ───────────────
1239: 
1240:         internal void HandleProcessMessage(CefProcessMessage message)
1241:         {
1242:             var name = message.Name;
1243: 
1244:             switch (name)
1245:             {
1246:                 case "JsEvaluationResult":
1247:                     HandleJsEvaluationResult(message);
1248:                     break;
1249: 
1250:                 case "NativeObjectCallRequest":
1251:                     HandleNativeObjectCallRequest(message);
1252:                     break;
1253: 
1254:                 default:
1255:                     // JsContextCreated / JsContextReleased / UnhandledException — ignore
1256:                     break;
1257:             }
1258:         }
1259: 
1260:         private void HandleJsEvaluationResult(CefProcessMessage message)
1261:         {
1262:             int taskId;
1263:             bool success;
1264:             string resultJson;
1265:             string exception;
1266: 
1267:             using (var args = message.Arguments)
1268:             {
1269:                 taskId = args.GetInt(0);
1270:                 success = args.GetBool(1);
1271:                 resultJson = args.GetString(2);
1272:                 exception = args.GetString(3);
1273:             }
1274: 
1275:             if (_pendingEvals.TryRemove(taskId, out var tcs))
1276:             {
1277:                 if (success)
1278:                     tcs.TrySetResult(resultJson);
1279:                 else
1280:                     tcs.TrySetException(new Exception(exception ?? "Unknown JS error"));
1281:             }
1282:         }
1283: 
1284:         private void HandleNativeObjectCallRequest(CefProcessMessage message)
1285:         {
1286:             int callId;
1287:             string objectName;
1288:             string memberName;
1289:             string argsJson;
1290: 
1291:             using (var args = message.Arguments)
1292:             {
1293:                 callId = args.GetInt(0);
1294:                 objectName = args.GetString(1);
1295:                 memberName = args.GetString(2);
1296:                 argsJson = args.GetString(3);
1297:             }
1298: 
1299:             if (!_registeredObjects.TryGetValue(objectName, out var reg))
1300:             {
1301:                 SendNativeObjectCallResult(callId, null, $"Object '{objectName}' not registered");
1302:                 return;
1303:             }
1304: 
1305:             if (!reg.Methods.TryGetValue(memberName, out var method))
1306:             {
1307:                 SendNativeObjectCallResult(callId, null, $"Method '{memberName}' not found on '{objectName}'");
1308:                 return;
1309:             }
1310: 
1311:             object result = null;
1312:             Exception ex = null;
1313: 
1314:             try
1315:             {
1316:                 var parameters = method.GetParameters();
1317:                 var invokeArgs = DeserializeCallArgs(argsJson, parameters);
1318:                 result = method.Invoke(reg.Target, invokeArgs);
1319: 
1320:                 // Handle Task return values — wait for completion
1321:                 if (result is Task task)
1322:                 {
1323:                     task.ContinueWith(t =>
1324:                     {
1325:                         object taskResult = null;
1326:                         Exception taskEx = null;
1327:                         try
1328:                         {
1329:                             // Reflection to get Task<T>.Result
1330:                             var resultProp = t.GetType().GetProperty("Result");
1331:                             if (resultProp != null)
1332:                                 taskResult = resultProp.GetValue(t);
1333:                         }
1334:                         catch (Exception e)
1335:                         {
1336:                             taskEx = e.InnerException ?? e;
1337:                         }
1338: 
1339:                         if (taskEx != null)
1340:                             SendNativeObjectCallResult(callId, null, taskEx.Message);
1341:                         else
1342:                             SendNativeObjectCallResult(callId, taskResult, null);
1343:                     });
1344:                     return; // result will be sent asynchronously
1345:                 }
1346:             }
1347:             catch (Exception e)
1348:             {
1349:                 ex = e.InnerException ?? e;
1350:             }
1351: 
1352:             SendNativeObjectCallResult(callId, result, ex?.Message);
1353:         }

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the transparency situation. Let me synthesize this:

**GDCefGlue has TWO rendering modes:**

1. **OSR mode** (default, `UseEmbeddedWindow = false`):
   - `SetAsWindowless(IntPtr.Zero, Transparent)` 
   - CEF renders to memory → BGRA→RGBA conversion → Godot `ImageTexture`
   - Transparency is handled by CEF's windowless mode native alpha channel
   - The `Transparent` property controls the CEF `SetAsWindowless` parameter
   - In `_Draw()`, transparent mode uses `DrawTextureRect` without background fill

2. **Embedded window mode** (`UseEmbeddedWindow = true`):
   - `SetAsChild(godotHwnd, rect)` → CEF creates a real HWND child window
   - Transparency handled in `ProcessEmbeddedMode()` (in `CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs`)
   - Already removes `WS_CLIPCHILDREN` from the Godot window (like godot_wry does)
   - BUT: uses `LWA_COLORKEY` approach (black=transparent via `SetLayeredWindowAttributes`)
   - This requires the HTML page to have `body { background: #000000 }` — this is a key difference!

**The problem with the current embedded mode transparency approach (`LWA_COLORKEY`):**
- Uses color keying (black pixels become transparent) — not true alpha transparency
- Requires the HTML/CSS to be designed with black backgrounds to "show through"
- The WebView2Edge.cs approach is different:
  - `_webViewController.DefaultBackgroundColor = System.Drawing.Color.Transparent;` — this tells WebView2 to render with a transparent background natively
  - `SetWindowLong(webViewHandle, GWL_EXSTYLE, currentStyle | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT);` — this makes mouse clicks pass through (mouse event transparency)
  
So the user's point is:
1. godot_wry doesn't have a transparency problem because it uses the native system webview which handles transparency properly 
2. WebView2Edge.cs handles it differently by setting the WebView2 controller's DefaultBackgroundColor to Transparent (true alpha transparency), plus WS_EX_TRANSPARENT for mouse event passthrough

The GDCefGlue embedded mode currently uses `LWA_COLORKEY` which is a hack (only works with black-as-transparent), while CEF actually supports true transparency through its own mechanisms.

For CEF transparency in embedded mode, the approach should be different from WebView2:
- CEF's `SetAsChild()` renders with its own background
- For true transparency in CEF, there are few options:
  1. Use OSR mode (already works for transparent)
  2. In embedded mode, set CEF's background color to transparent via browser settings
  3. Use `SetLayeredWindowAttributes` with `LWA_ALPHA` for per-pixel alpha (requires the child window to be layered)

---

### 当前 GDCefGlue 的透明方案（两类模式）

#### 1. OSR 模式（默认，`UseEmbeddedWindow=false`）
- `SetAsWindowless(IntPtr.Zero, Transparent=true)` → CEF 渲染带 alpha 通道的内存像素
- `_Draw()` 中用 `DrawTextureRect(..., false)` 无背景绘制
- **问题**：CEF 在 OSR 模式下的透明效果并不完美——背景色仍可能显示为纯黑/白，需要对 CEF 的 `CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor` 做额外配置（设 `0x00000000` 才能实现真正透明）

#### 2. 嵌入窗口模式（`UseEmbeddedWindow=true`）— 当前实现
`CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs` 第63-82行：
```csharp
// 套路：LWA_COLORKEY（黑色=透明）
int exStyle = GetWindowLong(cefHwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE);
exStyle |= WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT;
SetWindowLong(cefHwnd, GWL_EXSTYLE, newExStyle);
SetLayeredWindowAttributes(cefHwnd, 0x000000, 0, LWA_COLORKEY);
```
**问题**：这是"伪透明"（颜色键控），**必须**要求 HTML 页面 `body { background: #000000 }` 黑色像素才透明，不能实现真正的 alpha 透明（半透明渐变阴影等）。

---

### 参考方案对比

| 方案 | 技术 | 透明类型 | 限制 |
|------|------|---------|------|
| **godot_wry** | 移除 `WS_CLIPCHILDREN` + 系统原生 WebView | 浏览器原生支持 | WebKit/WebView2 自身支持，无需额外操作 |
| **WebView2Edge.cs** | `DefaultBackgroundColor=Transparent` + `WS_EX_TRANSPARENT` | **真透明**（Per-pixel alpha） | WebView2 专用 API，简洁 |
| **GDCefGlue 嵌入模式（当前）** | `LWA_COLORKEY` 颜色键控 | **伪透明**（黑→透） | 需 HTML 黑色背景，不支持半透明 |

### WebView2Edge.cs 的核心差异

WebView2Edge.cs 的关键两步（比你当前的 `LWA_COLORKEY` 更干净）：

**① 引擎级透明**：
```csharp
_webViewController.DefaultBackgroundColor = System.Drawing.Color.Transparent;
```
→ WebView2 直接渲染透明背景，不需要 HTML 配合

**② 鼠标穿透**：
```csharp
SetWindowLong(webViewHandle, GWL_EXSTYLE, currentStyle | WS_EX_TRANSPARENT);
```
→ 使鼠标事件穿透透明区域到 Godot 控件

---

### CEF 下的透明方案

对于 CEF 嵌入窗口模式，不能用 WebView2 的 `DefaultBackgroundColor`（这是 WebView2 专有 API）。CEF 的可行方法：

1. **`CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor = 0x00000000`**（在 `CreateBrowser` 之前设置）— 告诉 CEF 使用透明背景渲染
2. **CEF 子窗口设置为 `WS_EX_LAYERED` + `SetLayeredWindowAttributes` 用 `LWA_ALPHA`**（不是 `LWA_COLORKEY`）— 但这需要子窗口是分层窗口，CEF 的 Chromium 子窗口**不支持**分层窗口样式（`WS_EX_LAYERED` 与 Chromium 渲染冲突）
3. **OSR 模式 + 透明背景**（实际最可靠的 CEF 透明方案）

所以 CEF 的嵌入模式透明本质上是**棘手的问题**——这就是为什么 CEF 生态中常见的是 OSR 模式实现透明，而非嵌入模式。相比之下 **godot_wry（WRY/WebView2）** 和 **WebView2Edge.cs** 没有这个问题，因为 WebView2 原生支持透明背景而无需走分层窗口这个弯路。

你现在的 `LWA_COLORKEY` 已经是嵌入模式下 CEF 能用的妥协方案了。如果想彻底解决，需要：
- **OSR 模式**下设置 `CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor = 0` 配合 `Transparent=true`
- 或放弃嵌入模式的透明，只用 OSR 做透明

Title: Chromium Embedded Framework (CEF): cef_browser_settings_t Struct Reference
URL: https://cef-builds.spotifycdn.com/docs/108.4/structcef__browser__settings__t.html
Published: N/A
Author: N/A
Highlights:
## ◆ background_color
...
END values that map to WebPreferences settings.
...
Background color used for the browser before a document is loaded and when no document color is specified. The alpha component must be either fully opaque (0xFF) or fully transparent (0x00). If the alpha component is fully opaque then the RGB components will be used as the background color. If the alpha component is fully transparent for a windowed browser then the CefSettings.background_color value will be used. If the alpha component is fully transparent for a windowless (off-screen) browser then transparent painting will be enabled.

Title: chrome: Add support for embedded non-Views windows · Issue #3294 · chromiumembedded/cef
URL: https://github.com/chromiumembedded/cef/issues/3294

Author: magreenblatt
Highlights:
## chrome: Add support for embedded non-Views windows
...
This issue involves the following functionality currently supported by the Alloy runtime:
...
- Embedded non-Views windows \(where the host application creates the top-level window\), created with `CefWindowInfo.SetAsChild`.
...
See issue [~~#2969~~](#2969/support-chrome-windows-with-cef-callbacks) for background on the Chrome runtime.
...
> chrome: win/linux: Add support for browser with native parent (see issue #3294)
> 
> This change adds Chrome runtime support on Windows and Linux for creating a
> browser parented to a native window supplied by the client application.
> Expected API usage and window behavior is similar to what already exists with
> the Alloy runtime. The parent window handle should be specified by using
> CefWindowInfo::SetAsChild in combination with the CefBrowserHost::CreateBrowser
> and CefLifeSpanHandler::OnBeforePopup callbacks.
> 
> The previously existing behavior of creating a fully-featured Chrome browser
> window when empty CefWindowInfo is used with CreateBrowser remains unchanged
> and Views is still the preferred API for creating top-level Chrome windows
> with custom styling (e.g. title bar only, frameless, etc).
> 
> The cefclient Popup Window test with a native parent window continues to crash
> on Linux with both the Alloy and Chrome runtimes (see issue #3165).
> 
> Also adds Chrome runtime support for CefDisplayHandler::OnCursorChange.
> 
> To test:
> 
> - Run `cefclient --enable-chrome-runtime [--use-views]` for the default (and
> 
> previously existing) Views-based behavior.
> 
> - Run `cefclient --enable-chrome-runtime --use-native` for the new native
> 
> parent window behavior.
> 
> - Run `cefclient --enable-chrome-runtime --use-native --no-activate` and the
> 
> window will not be activated (take input focus) on launch (Windows only).
> 
> - Run `cefclient --enable-chrome-runtime [--use-views|--use-native]
> 
> --mouse-cursor-change-disabled` and the mouse cursor will not change on
> mouseover of DOM elements.
>

Title: IWindowInfo Interface
URL: https://cefsharp.github.io/api/130.1.x/html/T_CefSharp_IWindowInfo.htm
Published: N/A
Author: N/A
Highlights:
| WindowlessRenderingEnabled | Set to true to create the browser using windowless (off-screen) rendering. No window will be created for the browser and all rendering will occur via the IRenderHandler interface. The ParentWindowHandle value will be used to identify monitor info and to act as the parent window for dialogs, context menus, etc. If | ParentWindowHandle is not provided then the main screen monitor will be used and some functionality that requires a parent window may not function correctly. In order to create windowless browsers the CefSettings.WindowlessRenderingEnabled value must be set to true. Transparent painting is enabled by default but can be disabled by setting BackgroundColor to an opaque value. |
...
| Name | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Dispose | Performs application-defined tasks associated with freeing, releasing, or resetting unmanaged resources.(Inherited from IDisposable) |
| SetAsChild(IntPtr) | Create the browser as a child window. Calls GetClientRect(Hwnd) to obtain the window bounds |
| SetAsChild(IntPtr, Rect) | Create the browser as a child window. |
| SetAsChild(IntPtr, Int32, Int32, Int32, Int32) | Create the browser as a child window. |
| SetAsPopup | Create the browser as a popup window. |
| SetAsWindowless | Create the browser using windowless (off-screen) rendering. No window will be created for the browser and all rendering will occur via the CefRenderHandler interface. This window will automatically be transparent unless a colored backgrond is set in the browser settings. |
| UnWrap | Used internally to get the underlying IWindowInfo instance. Unlikely you'll use this yourself. |

The |parent| value will be used to identify monitor info and to act as the parent window for dialogs, context menus, etc. If |parent| is not provided then the main screen monitor will be used and some functionality that requires a parent window may not function correctly. In order to create windowless browsers the CefSettings.windowless_rendering_enabled value must be set to true. Transparent painting is enabled by default but can be disabled by setting CefBrowserSettings.background_color to an opaque value.

Title: windows: Browser view doesn't work when embedded using `WS_EX_COMPOSITED`
URL: https://github.com/chromiumembedded/cef/issues/3562
Published: N/A
Author: N/A
Highlights:
# windows: Browser view doesn't work when embedded using `WS_EX_COMPOSITED`
...
If a window using `WS_EX_COMPOSITED` is passed to `CefWindowInfo::SetAsChild()`, the browser window doesn't work correctly, i.e. it doesn't repaint properly nor even dispatches some other events resulting in life-time-related problems (e.g. callbacks not being called) all of which disappear if `WS_EX_COMPOSITED` is removed. Of course, removing it results in flicker in the other controls that are children of the same top level window as the browser, so this is not ideal. 
 
Reproducing this problem in cefsimple would require writing a bit of Windows-specific code, but I could do it if it could be useful, i.e. if there is any chance of this limitation being lifted.

> Create the browser using windowless (off-screen) rendering. No window

Call this method to disable windowed rendering and to use
[RenderHandler](RenderHandler.md). See the pysdl2, screenshot, panda3d
and kivy examples.

In order to create windowless browsers the
ApplicationSettings.[windowless_rendering_enabled](ApplicationSettings.md#windowless_rendering_enabled)
value must be set to true.

You can pass 0 as `parentWindowHandle`, but then some things like
context menus and plugins may not display correctly.

...
Integration CEF into foreign applicationsThere is different possibilities exists: do it directly, or indirectly (implement out-of-process browser). I'm recommend always go with out-of-browser with foreign applications, especially if it is "pluggable" apps like Outlook or Visual Studio or whatever else, when you can easily disrupt host application stability or other plugin can disrupt your plugin.With ASP.NET situation even worse: application hosted in own app domain, life time is unknown (controlled externally by admin and app recycling policy), some thread stuff, so you even never be able to shutdown CEF correctly. Also CEF can do some things what will be not expected in asp.net hosting process. I'm already answer on similar question here, in short, it is easier with better net result is create separate browser process and interact with it. This is what i'm exactly do for Outlook before, and this is what i'm exactly do for tasks very similar to your's. Otherwise, for example, if browser crashed - it will crash with whole ASP.NET application, what will cause hard to debug errors.UPD, i'm just quote related part from link above:
...
fddima wrote:2. Implement out-of-process browser. I.e. plugin creates child process. Child process via IPC interact with plugin, and inserts native browser window by using window reparenting. Plugin simple wraps this. You can unify or leave unique each plugin. Hard way. But it guaranteed to work. There is how i'm make add-id for outlook. Additionally this can help break x86/x64 differences, while out of process browser can be simple x86 and able to run by x86 and x64 process. For outlook case this is also removes any process instability, so plugin does not conflict with anything, does not crash plugin host, and more important (with outlook case - no problems with stealed focus on new popup windows, no need to hook main hwnd (which finally almost impossible in outlook), something else...).1-st way are easier as for me.2-nd more reliable, but usually MUST be used if you not own host app.
...
I'm also wrote about somewhere at this forum, but general idea should be clear.CEF usage general points1. Read CEF wiki about CEF usage. It should give you understanding of CEF possibilities and common usage scenarios. CefGlue provides very similar API but some specifics exist. But i'm doesn't see that you need to know about specifics just now.2. Create minimal sample: in sense that it should be regular application (can be console - no matter) which do all work. It will be easier to getting started.3. You can inspect CefGlue's WpfOsr sample which actually uses windowless rendering (and then shows content to wpf's control). cefclient (C++) also supports windowless rendering (can be enabled by switch), so it can be used as reference sample implementation, but i'm recommend focus on something simplest - WpfOsr much more simpler (and limited) in comparison to cefclient, but this should be more than enough to get started.4. Enable at least error logging in CefSettings - sometimes it says something useful.Problems in your code (which i'm see with closed eyes)1. You should enable windowless rendering by using CefSettings::WindowlessRenderingEnabled.2. After browser created it should do lot of things, like fetch data from url via network, parse data and many other background things. So frame object is not appears immediately, and actually some time it still has zero frames. You should wait for example page finish load.General suggestions1. Avoid to using MultiThreadedMessageLoop without real need. Actually whenever you start new development, you not needed in this option. This option is windows only, and used to integrate CEF into existing applications, when you can't control message loop. Also current CEF versions supports external message pump mechanism for same reason, but without separate thread. But normally, you should use built-in message loop. It provides best performance, and better stability.2. Avoid to use CreateBrowserSync in real code. It is may be ok for sample code, but in all cases you want be truly asynchronous, what handled by CreateBrowser (creates browser asynchronously - and this is normal operation mode).

Create the browser using windowless (off-screen) rendering. No window will be created for the browser and all rendering will occur via the CefRenderHandler interface. This window will automatically be transparent unless a colored backgrond is set in the browser settings.
...
parentHandleType: SystemIntPtr Value will be used to identify monitor info and to act as the parent window for dialogs, context menus, etc. If not provided then the main screen monitor will be used and some functionality that requires a parent window may not function correctly. In order to create windowless browsers the CefSettings.windowless_rendering_enabled value must be set to true.

---

💭 **Reasoning:**

<details>
<summary>Click to expand reasoning</summary>

Okay, I've found very valuable information. Let me summarize the key findings:

**From CEF documentation (CefBrowserSettings.background_color):**
> "If the alpha component is fully transparent for a windowed browser then the CefSettings.background_color value will be used."

This means for `SetAsChild` (windowed mode), setting `CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor = 0x00000000` (fully transparent alpha) will NOT make the browser itself transparent - it just tells CEF to use the global `CefSettings.background_color` value instead. Only in OSR mode does it enable transparent painting.

**From CEF Issue #3891:**
There's a known bug (as of CEF 133) where `background-color` doesn't work correctly in windowed mode with the Chrome runtime. There's a patch that adds `web_view_background_visible` to force the background to be drawn.

**From CEF Forum (old but valuable):**
The user `efranco23` reported success with `SetTransparentPainting(TRUE)` + `SetAsChild(hWnd, rect)` by:
1. Creating the main window with `WS_EX_LAYERED`
2. Using `SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hWnd, RGB(0xff, 0xff, 0xff), 0xff, LWA_COLORKEY)` to remove white
3. But noted this is color-keying, not true transparency

**Key insight from the forum:**
> "It is definitely doesn't transparent background, just color replacing. By transparent background usually means that body { background-color: transparent; } and html engines draw it with true transparency. CEF inside doesn't do it for windowing mode"

So the fundamental truth is: **CEF's `SetAsChild` windowed mode does NOT support true per-pixel alpha transparency.** The Chromium HWND renders with a solid background.

Now let me think about what options exist:

### Option 1: LWA_COLORKEY (Current approach - color keying)
- Set CEF child window to `WS_EX_LAYERED` + `LWA_COLORKEY`
- Choose a specific color (e.g., black `0x000000`) as the transparency key
- HTML page must use that exact color for transparent areas
- **Pros**: Simple, works
- **Cons**: Not true alpha, HTML must cooperate, color bleeding risks

### Option 2: OSR mode + transparency (Already works)
- `SetAsWindowless` + `Transparent=true`
- CEF renders to memory with alpha channel
- Godot draws the texture with alpha
- **Pros**: True alpha transparency
- **Cons**: Higher CPU/GPU for memory copy, no hardware video acceleration in some cases

### Option 3: Hybrid approach (OSR for transparent content)
- Use OSR mode always (ignore embedded mode for transparent cases)
- The OSR texture already supports per-pixel alpha

### Option 4: DWM / WS_EX_LAYERED on the Godot window itself
- Set Godot's main window to `WS_EX_LAYERED` with `LWA_COLORKEY`
- The CEF child HWND renders normally, but the parent window can have transparency
- This is what the forum user did
- **Problem**: Layered window + child HWND = Chromium rendering issues (CEF Issue #3562 mentions `WS_EX_COMPOSITED` conflicts)

### Option 5: SetWindowRgn (Window region clipping)
- Instead of transparency, clip the CEF child window to a non-rectangular region
- Only useful for rounded corners, not for "holes" in the browser area

### Option 6: CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor = 0 in embedded mode
- In newer CEF versions (Alloy runtime, not Chrome runtime), setting `BackgroundColor = 0` might work
- In Chrome runtime, there's the bug from issue #3891

### Option 7: Chrome runtime + views framework
- `CefBrowserView::CreateBrowserView` supports transparent background correctly
- But this requires the Views framework, not SetAsChild
- Not suitable for embedding in Godot

### Option 8: The patch from CEF issue #3891
- A CEF source code patch that adds `web_view_background_visible` 
- Requires compiling CEF from source - not practical for a NuGet package

**The most practical options for GDCefGlue are:**

**Best approach for embedded mode: `CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor = 0x00000000` + LWA_COLORKEY**

Actually, let me re-read the CEF documentation more carefully:

"If the alpha component is fully transparent (0x00) for a windowed browser then the CefSettings.background_color value will be used."

So for SetAsChild (windowed), BackgroundColor=0 just defers to CefSettings.background_color (global). This means CEF explicitly does NOT support per-pixel alpha transparency in windowed mode through this API.

The only real options are:
1. **LWA_COLORKEY** - color keying (fake transparency, current approach)
2. **SetWindowRgn** - window region (not really transparency)
3. **DWM composition** - `DwmExtendFrameIntoClientArea` (extends Aero glass into the client area)
4. **OSR mode** - the only way to get true per-pixel alpha

Let me provide a comprehensive answer.

Actually, one more thing - I should also check if there's a way with `CefRuntimeStyle.Alloy` vs `CefRuntimeStyle.Chrome`. The Alloy runtime (older) might handle this differently from the Chrome runtime.

Also, let me look at the CefGlue WindowInfo.cs to see if `SetTransparentPainting` still exists or maps to something.

</details>

现在看清了全貌。以下是我对 CEF 嵌入窗口模式下透明度方案的完整分析。

---

## CEF `SetAsChild` 嵌入窗口模式透明方案分析

### 核心事实

CEF 官方文档明确指出：
> *"If the alpha component is fully transparent (0x00) for a **windowed browser** then the `CefSettings.background_color` value will be used."*

这意味着 **CEF 的 `SetAsChild` 窗口模式本身不支持 per-pixel alpha 透明**。`BackgroundColor` 设 `0x00000000` 在窗口模式下不会开启透明——它只是回退到全局设置，不像 OSR 模式那样直接启用 alpha 通道渲染。这和 WebView2 的 `DefaultBackgroundColor = Color.Transparent`（原生支持 per-pixel alpha）有本质区别。

---

### 方案对比

| 方案 | 透明类型 | 实现难度 | 局限性 | 推荐度 |
|------|---------|---------|--------|-------|
| **① LWA_COLORKEY 改进版** | 颜色键控 | 低 | 需 HTML 配合，不支持半透明 | ⭐⭐⭐ |
| **② CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor** | 伪透明 | 低 | Chrome runtime 下 CEF #3891 有 bug | ⭐⭐ |
| **③ OSR 模式** | 真 alpha | 中 | 无硬件视频加速 | ⭐⭐⭐⭐ |
| **④ 混合渲染（OSR + 嵌入）** | 可定制 | 高 | 架构复杂 | ⭐⭐ |
| **⑤ 放弃 CEF，用 WebView2** | 真 alpha | 重 | 功能不如 CEF 完整 | 看场景 |

---

### 方案详解

#### 方案①：LWA_COLORKEY 改进版（当前路径，推荐改进）

你现在 `CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs` 第63-82行已经实现了基础版。可以针对 CEF 做以下优化：

**关键差异——CEF 比 WebView2 多一件事**：必须同步设置 `CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor` 为键控色，否则 CEF 渲染白色背景时，键控色会被页面背景覆盖：

```csharp
// CreateBrowser 之前
var settings = new CefBrowserSettings
{
    WindowlessFrameRate = frameRate,
    BackgroundColor = 0x00000001  // ARGB: alpha=00, R=0, G=0, B=1
};
```

**同时**：
- **用 `RGB(1,1,1)`（极黑）代替纯黑 `0x000000`** 做键控色——避免与页面中真正的黑色内容冲突（文字、阴影等）
- **注入 JS** `document.body.style.background = 'rgb(1,1,1)'` 为无背景色页面设置键控色
- 对 CEF 子 HWND 设 `WS_EX_LAYERED | LWA_COLORKEY` 用该键控色

这样即使页面有纯黑文字 `color: #000`，也不会"被透明掉"，因为键控色是 `rgb(1,1,1)` 而非 `rgb(0,0,0)`。

#### 方案②：CefBrowserSettings.BackgroundColor = 0

直接改 `CreateBrowser`：

```csharp
var settings = new CefBrowserSettings
{
    WindowlessFrameRate = frameRate,
    BackgroundColor = 0  // 0x00000000
};
```

CEF Alloy runtime 下可能有效，但你的项目用的是 CEF 149 + **Chrome runtime**（CefGlue.Common 默认启用），CEF Issue #3891 指出 Chrome runtime 下 `--use-native` 模式背景色有问题——Chrome runtime 内部会调用 `contents_web_view()->SetBackgroundVisible(false)` 覆盖掉你的设置。官方 patch 需要额外加 `web_view_background_visible` 字段，新版 CEF 才可能修复。

你可以先试试——如果 Chrome runtime 下无效，切到 Alloy runtime 可能有效，但 Alloy 不支持扩展等 Chrome 特性。

#### 方案③：OSR 模式（推荐——需要真透明时）

这是 CEF 唯一原生支持 per-pixel alpha 的路径。你现在 OSR 模式已实现 `SetAsWindowless(IntPtr.Zero, Transparent)` + `OnPaint` → Godot 纹理。

**OSR 的主要问题**：视频/WebGL 性能不如嵌入模式（因为每帧内存拷贝）。
**但改进方案**：使用 CEF 149 的共享内存机制（`CefSharedMemoryRegion`）代替 `Marshal.Copy`，可以大幅降低 OSR 拷贝开销。

#### 方案④：混合渲染

参考 `BRIDGE_TODO.md` 中的思路，`UseEmbeddedWindow` 双模式可选：
- **透明页面** → OSR 模式
- **非透明页面** → 嵌入模式

从 `_Ready()` 根据 `Transparent` 属性自动切换，用户不感知。

#### 方案⑤：WebView2 混合（如果 CEF 透明无解）

如果应用场景主要是透明 UI（而非需要 CEF 完整 Chromium 功能），可以考虑：
- **CEF 嵌入模式**做不透明主内容（视频、WebGL）
- **WebView2**新建透明窗口做 UI 层——利用 `DefaultBackgroundColor = Transparent` 原生支持

---

### 代码修改建议

针对方案①（最实用），改进 `CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs`：

```csharp
private const uint TransparencyKeyColor = 0x00010101; // RGB(1,1,1) 代替纯黑

private void ApplyCefWindowedTransparency(IntPtr cefHwnd)
{
    if (!Transparent) return;
    
    // 设置 CEF 子窗口的分层样式
    int exStyle = NativeWindowMethods.GetWindowLong(cefHwnd, NativeWindowMethods.GWL_EXSTYLE);
    int newExStyle = exStyle | NativeWindowMethods.WS_EX_LAYERED;
    NativeWindowMethods.SetWindowLong(cefHwnd, NativeWindowMethods.GWL_EXSTYLE, newExStyle);
    
    // LWA_COLORKEY: 把 RGB(1,1,1) 像素变透明
    NativeWindowMethods.SetLayeredWindowAttributes(
        cefHwnd,
        TransparencyKeyColor,   // crKey: 0x00BBGGRR = 0x00010101
        0,
        NativeWindowMethods.LWA_COLORKEY);
}
```

同时 `CreateBrowser` 里：

```csharp
var settings = new CefBrowserSettings
{
    WindowlessFrameRate = frameRate,
};

if (Transparent && _embeddedMode)
{
    // 窗口模式下 BackgroundColor alpha=0 会回退到 CefSettings 全局色
    // 但设置键控色可以让 CEF 渲染该颜色，再由 LWA_COLORKEY 去掉
    settings.BackgroundColor = TransparencyKeyColor | 0xFF000000; // opaque RGB(1,1,1)
}
```

---

### 总结

| 你的需求 | 方案 |
|---------|------|
| 嵌入模式下简单透明（背景纯色镂空） | **方案① LWA_COLORKEY 改进版**——`RGB(1,1,1)` 键控 + `BackgroundColor` 同步 |
| 真 alpha 透明（半透明、渐变） | **方案③ OSR 模式**——CEF 唯一原生支持 |
| 两者兼顾 | **方案④ 双模式自动切换**——透明→OSR，不透明→嵌入 |
| 彻底解决透明问题 | 换 WebView2——但会失去 CEF 的完整Chromium能力 |

要我帮你实现在 `CefGlueControl.Embedded.cs` 中方案①的改进吗？还是需要评估 OSR 模式的透明效果具体哪里有问题？

<details>
<summary>Click to expand reasoning</summary>

The user is asking a direct question: "Does CEF itself not support transparency like WebView does?"

Let me give a clear, concise answer based on what I've learned from the research.

The short answer is: **Yes, that's correct. CEF's `SetAsChild` (windowed/embedded) mode does NOT support per-pixel alpha transparency like WebView2 does.** 

But I should be precise and nuanced:

1. **CEF OSR mode (SetAsWindowless)**: DOES support true per-pixel alpha transparency. The alpha channel from the web page rendering is preserved in the pixel buffer. This is what your current OSR mode with `Transparent=true` already does.

2. **CEF embedded mode (SetAsChild)**: Does NOT support true per-pixel alpha. The Chromium HWND renders with a solid background. The `BackgroundColor` setting in windowed mode just controls the fallback color, not alpha transparency. `LWA_COLORKEY` is the only practical workaround, and it's color-keying, not alpha.

3. **WebView2**: Has `DefaultBackgroundColor = Color.Transparent` which is a native WebView2 API that enables per-pixel alpha rendering even in windowed/embedded mode. CEF has no equivalent API.

4. **godot_wry (WRY)**: Uses the system's native WebView (WebView2 on Windows, WebKit on Mac), which all support transparency natively in embedded mode.

So the fundamental issue is a **CEF/Chromium architecture limitation** - Chromium's windowed rendering path (the one used by `SetAsChild`) doesn't support alpha transparency. Only the off-screen rendering path does. WebView2, being a different product with a different API surface, implemented this natively.

Let me also check: Chromium itself has this limitation? Yes, it's the same Chromium under the hood. WebView2 is also based on Chromium, but Microsoft added the `DefaultBackgroundColor` API that handles the compositing differently. In fact, WebView2 on Windows uses a similar approach internally - it uses visual compositing (DirectComposition/DWM) rather than a child HWND, which allows per-pixel alpha.

Actually, to be more precise: WebView2 doesn't use `SetAsChild` (child HWND) mode at all. It uses visual hosting APIs (IDCompositionVisual or Windows.UI.Composition) which provide compositing support with alpha transparency. CEF's `SetAsChild` is the old HWND-based approach which doesn't support this.

CEF does have a visual hosting mode too (via `CefWindowInfo.SetAsWindowless` + DComp), but it's positioned as a variant of OSR, not as an embedded window mode. In CefGlue, this would be through `SetAsWindowless` with a real parent HWND handle.

So the real deep answer is: **CEF CAN do transparent rendering, but ONLY through OSR/windowless mode, not through the child HWND (SetAsChild) mode. WebView2 uses a different hosting model (visual compositing) that supports transparency in all modes.**

Let me keep the answer concise and clear.

</details>

对。**CEF 的嵌入窗口模式（`SetAsChild`）在架构上就不支持真透明**，这不是配置问题，而是 Chromium 窗口渲染管线的根本限制。

### 为什么 CEF 不行，WebView2 行？

两者底层都是 Chromium，但**托管方式不同**：

| | CEF `SetAsChild` | WebView2 | CEF OSR (`SetAsWindowless`) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **托管方式** | 子 HWND（窗口句柄） | DirectComposition 视觉合成 | CPU 内存绘制 |
| **透明支持** | ❌ Chromium HWND 不支持 alpha | ✅ 合成器支持 per-pixel alpha | ✅ 像素自带 alpha |
| **硬件加速** | ✅ 完整 GPU | ✅ 完整 GPU | ⚠️ 部分受限 |

WebView2 内部根本**不走子 HWND 模式**——它用 `IDCompositionVisual`（DirectComposition）或 `Windows.UI.Composition`，这些视觉层原生支持 alpha 透明。微软为 WebView2 加的 `DefaultBackgroundColor = Transparent` 就是控制合成器的背景色。

CEF 也有类似的合成模式 —— `SetAsWindowless` 传真实父窗口句柄 + DComp 后端——
但 CefGlue 把这归类为 **OSR 变体**（仍然走 `CefRenderHandler.OnPaint`），与 `SetAsChild` 的完全硬件加速渲染是两条路。

### 一句话

**CEF 嵌入窗口要真透明，只有一个出路：不走 `SetAsChild`，走 OSR。** 这就是为什么你项目的 OSR 模式透明没问题，而嵌入模式只能用 `LWA_COLORKEY` 颜色键控这种"伪透明"。

不是你没配置对，是 CEF 架构就没给 `SetAsChild` 开这条路。WebView2/godot_wry 没这问题是因为它们压根不走 HWND 子窗口渲染这条路。

4: /// <summary>
5: /// i3D Export 特性演示 — 全局类，可直接作为节点添加。
6: ///
7: /// 三大核心特性：

18:     //
19:     //  Inspector 中勾选/取消 bool 时，setter 调用

31:     [Export]
32:     public bool UseDetailedStats
33:     {
34:         get => _useDetailedStats;

43:         get => _useElementResistance;

45:     }
46: 
47: 
48:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
49:     //  特性二：[ExportGroup] 分组
50:     //  特性三：[ExportSubgroup] 嵌套子分组
51:     //
52:     //  将属性归类为 Inspector 可折叠组、子组二级结构：
53:     //    ▸ 角色基础属性
54:     //      · 生命值 / 移动速度 / 角色名字
55:     //      ▸ 战斗属性

61:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
62: 
63:     [ExportGroup("角色基础属性")]
64: 
65:     [Export(PropertyHint.Range, "1,999")]

67:

70: 
71:     [Export]

73: 
74:     [ExportSubgroup("战斗属性")]

76:     [Export(PropertyHint.Range, "0,999")]

85: 
86:     // ---- 以下子组内的属性受 bool 控制动态显隐 ----
87: 
88:     [ExportSubgroup("高级战斗属性")]
89:     [Export(PropertyHint.Range, "0,100")]
90:     public float CriticalRate { get; set; } = 5.0f;
91: 
92:     [Export(PropertyHint.Range, "100,500")]

94: 
95:     [Export(PropertyHint.Range, "0,100")]

97: 
98:     [ExportSubgroup("元素抗性")]

101:

108: 
109:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

111:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
112: 
113:     [ExportGroup("资源引用")]
114: 
115:     [Export(PropertyHint.File, "*.json")]
116:     public string ConfigFilePath { get; set; } = "";
117: 
118:     [Export(PropertyHint.Dir)]

120: 
121:     [Export(PropertyHint.ColorNoAlpha)]

123:

126:

128:     public int ElementFlags { get; set; } = 0;

132:     //  Inspector 动态属性刷新 —— 核心机制
133:     //
134:     //  NotifyPropertyListChanged() 触发后，
135:     //  引擎遍历所有 Export 属性调用 _ValidateProperty()，
136:     //  在此根据 bool 状态设置 property["usage"] 来控制显隐。
137:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
138: 
139:     public override void _ValidateProperty(Dictionary property)
140:     {
141:         var propName = property["name"].AsStringName();
142: 
143:         // 控制「高级战斗属性」子组内的属性显隐
144:         if (propName == "高级战斗属性" ||
145:             propName == "CriticalRate" ||
146:             propName == "CriticalDamage" ||
147:             propName == "AttackSpeedBonus")
148:         {
149:             if (!UseDetailedStats)
150:             {
151:                 property["usage"] = (int)PropertyUsageFlags.NoEditor;
152:             }
153:         }
154: 
155:         // 控制「元素抗性」子组内的属性显隐
156:         if (propName == "元素抗性" ||
157:             propName == "FireResistance" ||
158:             propName == "IceResistance" ||
159:             propName == "LightningResistance")
160:         {
161:             if (!UseElementResistance)
162:             {
163:                 property["usage"] = (int)PropertyUsageFlags.NoEditor;
164:             }
165:         }
166:     }
167: 
168: 
169:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
170:     //  运行时逻辑：验证 Export 值是否正确反映
171:     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
172: 
173:     public override void iStart()
174:     {
175:         S3.Print("════════════════════════════════");
176:         S3.Print("[ExportDemo] 初始化完成 —— 当前配置：");
177:         S3.Print($"  UseDetailedStats = {UseDetailedStats}");
178:         S3.Print($"  UseElementResistance = {UseElementResistance}");
179:         S3.Print($"  角色: {CharacterName}, HP={Health}, SPD={Speed}");

1: using System;
2: using System.Text.Json;
3: using Godot;
4: 
5: namespace GDCefGlue
6: {
7:     // ── 事件穿透（JS → C# → Godot）— partial class 拆分 ─────────────
8:     // 参考 godot_wry 的 JS 事件监听 + IPC 转发模式
9:     public partial class CefGlueControl
10:     {
11:         /// <summary>
12:         /// 事件转发 JS。注入到每个页面，通过 godot://bridge 机制将浏览器内的
13:         /// 鼠标/键盘事件转发到 Godot，实现事件穿透。
14:         ///
15:         /// 使用单个隐藏 iframe 复用，避免每帧创建/销毁 DOM 元素。
16:         /// 参考 godot_wry 的 JS 事件监听 + IPC 转发模式。
17:         /// </summary>
18:         private const string EventForwardingScript = @"
19: (function(){
20:     if (window.__godotEventForwardInjected) return;
21:     window.__godotEventForwardInjected = true;
22: 
23:     // 创建单个隐藏 iframe 用于 godot://bridge 通信
24:     var _f = document.createElement('iframe');
25:     _f.style.cssText = 'display:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;border:none!important';
26:     document.documentElement.appendChild(_f);
27: 
28:     function godotEvent(type, data) {
29:         var p = {eventType: type};
30:         for (var k in data) { p[k] = data[k]; }
31:         _f.src = 'godot://bridge?type=event_forward&payload=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(p));
32:     }
33: 
34:     // ── 鼠标事件 ──
35:     document.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
36:         godotEvent('mouse_down', {

79: ";
80: 
81:         /// <summary>
82:         /// 在页面加载完成后注入事件转发 JS（仅嵌入模式）。
83:         /// 在 OnLoadEnd 中调用，确保每个页面都有事件转发能力。
84:         /// </summary>
85:         internal void InjectEventForwardingScriptIfNeeded()
86:         {
87:             if (!_embeddedMode || _browser == null)
88:                 return;
89: 
90:             var frame = _browser.GetMainFrame();
91:             if (frame != null)
92:             {
93:                 frame.ExecuteJavaScript(EventForwardingScript, "godot://event_forward", 0);
94:             }
95:         }
96: 
97:         /// <summary>
98:         /// 处理从 JS 转发过来的事件，构造 Godot InputEvent 并推回 Godot 事件系统。
99:         /// 坐标映射：JS clientX/clientY（CEF 物理像素）→ Godot 虚拟像素坐标
100:         ///   godotX = control.GlobalPosition.X + (cefX / contentScale)
101:         ///   godotY = control.GlobalPosition.Y + (cefY / contentScale)
102:         /// </summary>
103:         internal void HandleForwardedEvent(string payload)
104:         {
105:             if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(payload) || _browserHost == null)
106:                 return;
107: 
108:             try
109:             {
110:                 using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(payload);
111:                 var root = doc.RootElement;
112: 
113:                 string eventType = root.GetProperty("eventType").GetString();
114: 
115:                 switch (eventType)
116:                 {
117:                     case "mouse_down":
118:                     case "mouse_up":
119:                         HandleForwardedMouseButton(root, eventType == "mouse_down");
120:                         break;
121:                     case "mouse_move":
122:                         HandleForwardedMouseMove(root);
123:                         break;
124:                     case "mouse_wheel":
125:                         HandleForwardedMouseWheel(root);
126:                         break;
127:                 }
128:             }
129:             catch (Exception ex)
130:             {
131:                 GD.PrintErr($"[CefGlueControl] Failed to handle forwarded event: {ex.Message}");
132:             }
133:         }
134: 
135:         /// <summary>
136:         /// 将 CEF 浏览器内的物理像素坐标转换为 Godot 虚拟像素坐标。
137:         /// </summary>
138:         private static Vector2 CefToGodotCoord(float cefX, float cefY, Vector2 controlGlobalPos, float contentScale)
139:         {
140:             return new Vector2(controlGlobalPos.X + (cefX / contentScale), controlGlobalPos.Y + (cefY / contentScale));
141:         }
142: 
143:         private void HandleForwardedMouseButton(JsonElement root, bool pressed)
144:         {
145:             int button = root.GetProperty("button").GetInt32();
146:             float cefX = root.GetProperty("x").GetSingle();
147:             float cefY = root.GetProperty("y").GetSingle();
148:             var godotPos = CefToGodotCoord(cefX, cefY, _cachedGlobalPosition, _cachedContentScale);
149: 
150:             var evt = new InputEventMouseButton
151:             {
152:                 Pressed = pressed,
153:                 Position = godotPos,
154:                 GlobalPosition = godotPos,
155:                 ButtonIndex = button switch
156:                 {
157:                     0 => MouseButton.Left,
158:                     1 => MouseButton.Middle,
159:                     2 => MouseButton.Right,
160:                     _ => MouseButton.Left
161:                 },
162:                 ShiftPressed = root.TryGetProperty("shift", out var s) && s.GetBoolean(),
163:                 CtrlPressed = root.TryGetProperty("ctrl", out var c) && c.GetBoolean(),
164:                 AltPressed = root.TryGetProperty("alt", out var a) && a.GetBoolean()
165:             };
166: 
167:             CallDeferred(nameof(PushInputEvent), evt);
168:         }
169: 
170:         private void HandleForwardedMouseMove(JsonElement root)
171:         {
172:             float cefX = root.GetProperty("x").GetSingle();
173:             float cefY = root.GetProperty("y").GetSingle();
174:             var godotPos = CefToGodotCoord(cefX, cefY, _cachedGlobalPosition, _cachedContentScale);
175: 
176:             var evt = new InputEventMouseMotion
177:             {
178:                 Position = godotPos,
179:                 GlobalPosition = godotPos,
180:                 Relative = Vector2.Zero,
181:                 ShiftPressed = root.TryGetProperty("shift", out var s) && s.GetBoolean(),
182:                 CtrlPressed = root.TryGetProperty("ctrl", out var c) && c.GetBoolean(),
183:                 AltPressed = root.TryGetProperty("alt", out var a) && a.GetBoolean()
184:             };
185: 
186:             CallDeferred(nameof(PushInputEvent), evt);
187:         }
188: 
189:         private void HandleForwardedMouseWheel(JsonElement root)
190:         {
191:             float deltaY = root.GetProperty("deltaY").GetSingle();
192:             float cefX = root.GetProperty("x").GetSingle();
193:             float cefY = root.GetProperty("y").GetSingle();
194:             var godotPos = CefToGodotCoord(cefX, cefY, _cachedGlobalPosition, _cachedContentScale);
195: 
196:             var evt = new InputEventMouseButton
197:             {
198:                 Position = godotPos,
199:                 GlobalPosition = godotPos,
200:                 ButtonIndex = deltaY > 0 ? MouseButton.WheelUp : MouseButton.WheelDown,
201:                 Pressed = true,
202:                 Factor = Math.Abs(deltaY) / 120.0f
203:             };
204: 
205:             // 先触按下，再触释放（模拟滚动的一次性脉冲）
206:             CallDeferred(nameof(PushInputEvent), evt);
207:             var evtUp = new InputEventMouseButton
208:             {
209:                 Position = godotPos,
210:                 GlobalPosition = godotPos,
211:                 ButtonIndex = evt.ButtonIndex,
212:                 Pressed = false,
213:                 Factor = evt.Factor
214:             };
215:             CallDeferred(nameof(PushInputEvent), evtUp);
216:         }
217: 
218:         private void PushInputEvent(InputEvent evt)
219:         {
220:             var viewport = GetViewport();
221:             if (viewport != null)
222:             {
223:                 viewport.PushInput(evt);
224:             }
225:         }
226:     }
227: }

(End of file - total 227 lines)
</content>
```

47:     ///   fetch('godot://bridge?type=ping&cb=test1&payload={}').catch(()=>{})
48:     ///   var i=document.createElement('iframe'); i.src='godot://bridge?type=ping&cb=test1&payload=%7B%7D'; document.body.appendChild(i);
49:     /// Or if _godotBridge is injected:

51:     /// </summary>
52:     private void OnBridgeRequest(string type, string payload, string cbId)
53:     {
54:         GD.Print($"[Bridge] type={type}, cb={cbId ?? "none"}, payload={payload}");
55: 
56:         switch (type)
57:         {
58:             case "ping":
59:                 _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"status\":\"pong\"}");
60:                 CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Bridge: ping → pong");
61:                 break;
62: 
63:             case "status":
64:                 var status = $"{{\"initialized\":{_browser.IsBrowserInitialized.ToString().ToLower()},\"loading\":{_browser.IsLoading.ToString().ToLower()},\"title\":\"{_browser.Title}\"}}";
65:                 _browser.SendResponse(cbId, status);
66:                 break;
67:

69:                 var json = Json.ParseString(payload);
70:                 if (json.VariantType == Variant.Type.Dictionary)
71:                 {
72:                     var dict = json.AsGodotDictionary();
73:                     string url = dict.ContainsKey("url") ? dict["url"].AsString() : "";
74:                     if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
75:                     {
76:                         CallDeferred(nameof(NavigateFromBridge), url);
77:                         _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"status\":\"navigating\"}");
78:                     }
79:                     else
80:                     {
81:                         _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"error\":\"url is empty\"}");
82:                     }
83:                 }
84:                 break;
85: 
86:             default:
87:                 GD.PrintErr($"[Bridge] Unknown type: {type}");
88:                 _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"error\":\"unknown type\"}");
89:                 break;
90:         }
91:     }

In Godot, ExportGroup/ExportSubgroup work by setting a group name before properties. When the group name property is hidden with `_ValidateProperty`, the entire group is hidden in the inspector.

So I need to hide the group name "Embedded Mode" when Mode != EmbeddedWindow, which will collapse the entire group. Similarly, I can hide the ForwardInputEvents property.

Let me add the `_ValidateProperty` method. I need to find a good place for it in the file - maybe near the end, before the methods like `ShowDeveloperTools`.

Actually, let me also check - the Mode setter also needs to update the `_renderMode` field and `ActiveRenderMode` static, but those are set in `_Ready()`. Since `NotifyPropertyListChanged()` in the setter will cause the inspector to re-query, changing Mode at runtime in the editor should also update the effective render mode... but the user said it should be set "before the browser is created". So the `_Ready` method will copy Mode to `_renderMode` and `ActiveRenderMode`. Changing it in the editor at design time will cause `_ValidateProperty` to fire and hide the group.

Let me add the `_ValidateProperty` method. I'll place it just before `ShowDeveloperTools`.

252:     private void LogDeferred(string text)
253:     {
254:         if (_log == null) return;

259:     private void LogErrDeferred(string text)
260:     {
261:         if (_log == null) return;
262:         _log.PushColor(new Color(1, 0.4f, 0.3f));
263:         _log.AddText(text);

<content>
1: # GDCefGlue JS-C# Bridge 演进 TODO
2: 
3: ## 现状
4: 
5: 当前 bridge 实现：`godot://bridge?type=X&cb=ID&payload=JSON` URL 拦截（`OnBeforeBrowse`）
6: 
7: - 方向：JS→C# 单向；C#→JS 靠 `ExecuteJavaScript` 回灌字符串
8: - 限制：
9:   - `OnBeforeBrowse` 无法读 POST body，payload 走 URL query（长度上限 ~2-64KB）
10:   - 每次 `sendToGodot` 新建 iframe，无复用，高频调用产生孤儿 DOM 节点
11:   - C#→JS 用字符串拼接 + 手写转义（`Replace("\\","\\\\")` x 5），有注入风险且每次触发 V8 parser
12:   - `BridgeRequest.Invoke` 在 CEF UI 线程同步执行，订阅者重活会卡渲染
13: 
14: ## CEF 原生 6 种 JS↔Native 机制（官方 API 实证）
15: 
16: 所有桥方案最终都建立在 CEF 底层 API 之上。CefGlue 完整暴露了以下 6 种机制的 C# 绑定。
17: 
18: | # | 机制 | 方向 | 同步/异步 | Payload 限制 | 线程 | 官方定位 |
19: |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
20: | 1 | `CefMessageRouterBrowserSide`（`window.cefQuery`） | JS→C++ 双向 | 异步（支持 `persistent` 推送） | 默认字符串；CEF 145+ 支持 binary + `CefSharedMemoryRegion`（可绕开 ~128KB IPC 限制） | UI 线程 | **官方推荐首选** |
21: | 2 | `CefRegisterExtension` + `CefV8Handler` | JS→C++（可同步返回） | 同步返回 `retval`，但运行在 Render 进程 | 无限制（CefV8Value 直接传） | TID_RENDERER | 静态全局 API |
22: | 3 | 窗口绑定 `OnContextCreated` + `CefV8Value::CreateFunction` | JS→C++ 同步 | 同步 | 无限制 | TID_RENDERER | 每帧可变、有 DOM |
23: | 4 | `CefFrame::ExecuteJavaScript` | C++→JS | 异步无返回 | 无返回值 | Browser 任意线程 / Renderer 主线程 | 一句话注入 |
24: | 5 | `CefFrame::SendProcessMessage` + `OnProcessMessageReceived` | 跨进程双向 | 异步 | `CefListValue` ~128KB；`CefSharedMemoryRegion` 无限 | UI 线程 | 底层 IPC 基石 |
25: | 6 | `CefResourceRequestHandler` / `CefSchemeHandlerFactory` | 双向（HTTP 语义） | 异步 | 无限制（流式） | TID_IO | 大 payload、二进制 |
26: 
27: ### 对你方案的映射
28: 
29: | 你当前的做法 | 对应 CEF 机制 | 问题 |
30: |---|---|---|
31: | `godot://` URL 拦截（`OnBeforeBrowse`） | 机制 5 的变体 | `OnBeforeBrowse` 无法读 POST body（机制 6 的 `OnBeforeResourceLoad` 才可以） |
32: | `iframe.src` 触发导航 | 机制 5（用 `SendProcessMessage` 语义） | 本质是"模拟导航发 IPC"，但多了 DOM 创建开销 |
33: | `ExecuteJavaScript` 回灌字符串 | 机制 4 的单向版 | 无返回值、每次 V8 parse、有注入风险 |
34: | 手搓 callback ID（`cb=ID`） | 机制 1 的 `cefQuery` 内建功能 | 官方 MessageRouter 已提供 callback ID 管理和 cancellation |
35: 
36: **结论**：你的方案在 CEF 原生坐标系里，把"应该用机制 1（MessageRouter）或机制 6（SchemeHandler）"的事情，用机制 5 的变体 + 机制 4 手搓实现了。这就是为什么主流库（CefSharp/CefGlue.Common/NanUI）都不走这条路。
37: 
38: ## 跨生态定位
39: 
40: | 维度 | 当前方案 | CefSharp 推荐 | Tauri | Electron |
41: |---|---|---|---|---|
42: | JS->Native transport | URL 拦截 | V8Handler + IPC | 自定义 scheme + HTTP POST | ipcMain/ipcRenderer |
43: | Native->JS transport | ExecuteJavaScript 字符串注入 | EvaluateScriptAsync | HTTP response | webContents.send |
44: | 双向 round trip | 需两条通道 | IPC 单通道 | HTTP 单通道 | IPC 单通道 |
45: | Payload 限制 | URL 长度 | ~128MB IPC | 无限（流式） | Structured Clone |
46: | 类型化契约 | 无 | 反射 + Binder | #[command] 宏 | 无（手搓） |
47: 
48: ## 演进路径对比
49: 
50: ### 路径 A：Tauri 方案（SchemeHandler + POST）【推荐】
51: 
52: 替换 `OnBeforeBrowse` 为 `OnBeforeResourceLoad` + `CefResourceHandler`，JS 侧改用 `fetch`。
53: 
54: 收益：
55: - 一次性拿到双向通道（HTTP request/response）
56: - payload 走 POST body，无 URL 长度限制
57: - 支持二进制（`application/octet-stream`）
58: - JS 侧 `fetch` 返回 Promise，符合现代 JS 习惯
59: - wrymium 项目已实证可行（0.48ms round trip）
60: 
61: 代价：
62: - `OnBeforeResourceLoad` 在 CEF IO 线程，不能阻塞，需改异步派发
63: - 需注册自定义 scheme（`CefRuntime.RegisterSchemeHandlerFactory`）
64: 
65: ### 路径 B：CefMessageRouter（官方方案）
66: 
67: 引入 CefGlue 自带的 `Wrapper/MessageRouter/`，JS 侧改用 `window.cefQuery`。
68: 
69: 收益：
70: - 官方稳定，cancellation / persistent push / shared memory 大 payload 全部现成
71: - persistent query 支持 C# 主动推 JS
72: 
73: 代价：
74: - 需转发 5 个生命周期回调（OnBeforeClose / OnBeforeBrowse / OnProcessMessageReceived / OnRenderProcessTerminated + renderer 侧 OnContextCreated/Released）
75: - payload 默认字符串，大 payload 要配置 message_size_threshold 启用 shared memory
76: 
77: ### 路径 C：CefGlue.Common 对象绑定
78: 
79: 引入 `CefGlue.BrowserProcess` + `CefGlue.Common` NuGet，用 `NativeObjectRegistry.Register`。
80: 
81: 收益：
82: - 零 transport 代码，直接反射调用 C# 方法
83: - 等价于 CefSharp `BindObjectAsync`
84: 
85: 代价：
86: - 增加 NuGet 依赖体积
87: - 只支持 JSON 字符串单参数（CefGlue.Common 限制）
88: - GDExtension AOT 场景需评估 trim 影响
89: 
90: ### 不推荐：Electron 方案
91: 
92: 等于手搓一遍 CefMessageRouter，且 CEF 无 contextBridge 隔离世界（Electron 核心卖点不可复现）。
93: 
94: ## 架构总览
95: 
96: ```
97: 可用方案：iframe → godot:// OnBeforeBrowse（当前，唯一可行方案）
98: 待解决：CefRuntime.RegisterExtension = False（NuGet 包不支持浏览器进程注册 V8）
99: ```
100: 
101: ### 当前限制
102: 
103: - `CefRuntime.RegisterExtension` 注册 V8 扩展失败（NuGet 版 CEF 不支持从浏览器进程注入）
104: - 无法使用 `CefMessageRouter`（需修改 BrowserProcess，独立项目不满足 addons 分发）
105: - 无法使用 `SchemeHandler + fetch`（file:// → ipc:// 跨域 CORS 死胡同）
106: - **唯一可行：iframe → OnBeforeBrowse 拦截**
107: 
108: ### 关键关注点：iframe 垃圾回收
109: 
110: 每次 `cefQuery` 调用会创建 iframe（`display:none`），当前方案 100ms 后自动移除。
111: 高频调用场景下（如 mousemove 60fps）需确保：
112: 
113: - [ ] iframe 创建后及时从 DOM 移除（已在 BridgeScript 中 100ms setTimeout 清理）
114: - [ ] 页面跳转/卸载时 iframe 不残留（CEF 自动清理页面 DOM，安全）
115: - [ ] 高频调用时 iframe 不堆积（当前 100ms 窗口期内不会堆积超过 ~6 个 iframe）
116: - [ ] 考虑复用单个隐藏 iframe 代替每次创建（后续优化点）
117: 
118: ### 渲染模式：双模式可选
119: 
120: ```
121: CefGlueControl 新增属性 UseEmbeddedWindow : bool
122:   ├── false [默认] → OSR 模式（当前实现不变）
123:   └── true → 嵌入窗口模式
124: ```
125: 
126: **OSR 模式（默认）**：
127: - `SetAsWindowless(IntPtr.Zero, Transparent)`
128: - `GodotRenderHandler.OnPaint` → Godot 纹理
129: - 事件由 Godot 原生处理，无需转发
130: 
131: **嵌入窗口模式（可选）**：
132: - `SetAsChild(hwnd)` → CEF 直接渲染到子 HWND
133: - 每帧 `GetGlobalRect()` → `SetWindowPos(cefHwnd, ...)` 位置同步
134: - 事件需 JS 监听转发（godot_wry 方案）
135: 
136: ### 事件透传方案（仅嵌入模式需要）
137: 
138: 参考 godot_wry 的 JS 事件转发模式：
139: 
140: ```
141: CEF 区域内鼠标/键盘操作
142:   → 注入的 JS 监听 DOM 事件（mousedown/mousemove/keydown...）
143:   → 通过 IPC 通道发送到 C#（_mouse_down, _mouse_up, _key_down...）
144:   → C# 构造 Godot InputEventXXX
145:   → viewport.push_input(event) 推回 Godot 事件系统
146: ```
147: 
148: 事件转发 IPC 通道选型：
149: 
150: | 方案 | 延迟 | 适用场景 |
151: |---|---|---|
152: | 同一个 SchemeHandler + fetch | ~0.5ms | 低频事件（click, keydown） |
153: | `CefRegisterExtension` + V8Handler | ~0.01ms | 高频事件（mousemove 60fps） |
154: | `CefMessageRouter` | ~0.1ms | 中频事件 |
155: 
156: **推荐**：事件转发走 SchemeHandler 同一通道（实现简单，减少学习成本），如果实测 mousemove 延迟过高再优化到 V8Handler。
157: 
158: ## 推荐路径：iframe bridge + cefQuery API TODO
159: 
160: ### 当前方案已验证
161: 
162: - [x] `window.cefQuery(request, callback)` API 在 JS 端可用
163: - [x] iframe → OnBeforeBrowse 拦截 → BridgeRequest 事件
164: - [x] C# → JS 响应通过 ExecuteJavaScript（`__cefQueryResponse`）
165: - [x] 测试全部通过（ping/echo/100KB payload）
166: - [x] 每页自动注入桥脚本（LoadEnd 事件注入）
167: - [x] 向后兼容旧 `_godotBridge` 回调
168: - [x] DevTool 按钮
169: 
170: ### 待优化
171: 
172: - [ ] iframe 复用：改用单个隐藏 iframe 反复设置 src，代替每次 createElement（减少 DOM 操作和 GC 压力）
173: - [ ] `CefRuntime.RegisterExtension = False` 持续关注 CefGlue 版本更新，未来可能支持
174: - [ ] 考虑 `SchemeHandler + fetch` 替代 iframe（需解决 file:// CORS，如用 `ipc://` 自加载页面）
175: 
176: ## 验收标准
177: 
178: - [ ] 现有 ping/status/navigate 测试用例通过（`MainUi.cs`）
179: - [ ] 大 payload（>10KB JSON）不再报 URL 长度错误
180: - [ ] round trip 延迟 < 5ms（实测）
181: - [ ] iframe 不再被创建（改用 `fetch`）
182: - [ ] C#->JS 推送仍可用（`SendToJs` 保留或升级）
183: - [ ] Godot 控件覆盖在 CEF 区域上时，鼠标事件仍可穿透到 Godot 控件（事件转发验证）
184: - [ ] 鼠标在 CEF 区域外点击 → CEF 失去焦点
185: - [ ] LSP 诊断无新增 error
186: - [ ] Godot 编辑器运行无报错
187: 
188: ## 参考实现
189: 
190: - **godot_wry**：https://github.com/doceazedo/godot_wry —— **核心参考**，嵌入窗口 + JS 事件转发 + IPC 的完整实现
191:   - `src/lib.rs`：JS 注入事件监听 + IPC handler + `push_input`
192:   - `src/godot_window.rs`：`DisplayServer.WindowGetNativeHandle` → HWND
193:   - 关键技巧：移除 `WS_CLIPCHILDREN`、坐标换算、`CURRENT_BUTTON_MASK` 状态机
194: - wrymium（Tauri+CEF 混合）：https://github.com/gxcsoccer/wrymium —— 用 `CefSchemeHandlerFactory` 实现 Tauri `invoke()`
195: - Chromely：https://github.com/chromelyapps/CefSharp —— 整个桥建立在 `CefSchemeHandlerFactory` 上
196: - CefSharp `ExampleResourceRequestHandler`：POST body 读取示例
197: - CefGlue 自带：`E:\Work\Hub\CefGlue\CefGlue\Classes.Handlers\CefResourceHandler.cs`
198: 
199: ## 关键 API 索引
200: 
201: | 用途 | CefGlue 类 | 文件 |
202: |---|---|---|
203: | 注册 scheme | `CefRuntime.RegisterSchemeHandlerFactory` | `CefGlue\CefRuntime.cs` |
204: | 注册 scheme（App 钩子） | `CefApp.OnRegisterCustomSchemes` | `CefGlue\Classes.Handlers\CefApp.cs` |
205: | Factory 接口 | `CefSchemeHandlerFactory` | `CefGlue\Classes.Handlers\CefSchemeHandlerFactory.cs` |
206: | Response handler | `CefResourceHandler` | `CefGlue\Classes.Handlers\CefResourceHandler.cs` |
207: | Request 拦截 | `CefResourceRequestHandler.OnBeforeResourceLoad` | `CefGlue\Classes.Handlers\CefResourceRequestHandler.cs` |
208: | MessageRouter（备选） | `CefMessageRouterBrowserSide` | `CefGlue\Wrapper\MessageRouter\` |
209: | 对象绑定（备选） | `NativeObjectRegistry` | `CefGlue.Common\ObjectBinding\` |

(End of file - total 209 lines)
